# Skills · T · part 46

855 entries, telcron-review-rubric to telegram-find. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **telcron-review-rubric**: How to review a Telcron change — the standard of approval, what to check in order (the database's rules and business rules, security and privacy, data integrity, tests, ownership and architecture, performance, readability), what blocks a merge versus what is a nit, and the verdict format. Load before reviewing any diff, before a QA or audit verdict, and before approving your own plan for a change. ([CodeGang007/telcron](https://github.com/CodeGang007/telcron/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/telcron-review-rubric))
- **telcron-security**: Telcron's security rules — identity and sessions, verification levels and roles, coins and fraud, ID documents, secrets, uploads, AI inputs and AWS access. Load before touching auth, any member-only route, the ledger, file uploads, a model prompt, or anything in AWS. ([CodeGang007/telcron](https://github.com/CodeGang007/telcron/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/telcron-security))
- **telcron-stack**: Telcron's exact stack and versions — Python, FastAPI, SQLAlchemy, Alembic, arq, React, Vite, Tailwind — and the choices that are settled. Load before adding a dependency, choosing a library, or when unsure which tool this repo uses for a job. ([CodeGang007/telcron](https://github.com/CodeGang007/telcron/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/telcron-stack))
- **telcron-testing**: How Telcron is tested — the levels (pure rules, integration against real PostgreSQL and Redis, the schema's own suite, drift, contract tests on recorded provider responses, the AI evaluation set, browser journeys, load, security), the tests every change must carry (access 401/403/404, pay-once, append-only, citations), the revert proof, and what not to test. Load before writing or reviewing tests, and before claiming a gate passed. ([CodeGang007/telcron](https://github.com/CodeGang007/telcron/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/telcron-testing))
- **telcron-user-flows**: What Telcron is for, who uses it, and the end-to-end journeys the product exists to serve — scan or search to passport, sign-up to Level 1, wallet and alerts, contributing and being paid in TelCoins, the safety team's queue, Level 2, business lookups — with what is built today, what is partial and what is phase two. Load BEFORE writing acceptance criteria, before designing or reviewing a screen or endpoint, and when deciding whether something "works". ([CodeGang007/telcron](https://github.com/CodeGang007/telcron/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/telcron-user-flows))
